# Skills · M · part 76

729 entries, minni to miro-doc. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **minni**: Use when the user asks the agent to recall, learn, coordinate, or operate through Minni, or when a task likely benefits from prior local memory and Living Repo Maps. Default automatic behavior is recall-only; do not learn unless explicitly requested. ([infektyd/minni](https://github.com/infektyd/minni/tree/HEAD/plugins/minni/.kilocode-plugin/skills/minni) · ★2)
- **minni-doctor**: Use when a Minni install misbehaves or its health is unknown — diagnose daemon/socket/DB, vault, plugin/MCP wiring, per-platform hooks, identity Layer 1, inbox lifecycle, correction re-injection, model providers, and plan injection. Doctor diagnoses and routes; minni-install repairs. ([infektyd/minni](https://github.com/infektyd/minni/tree/HEAD/plugins/minni/skills/minni-doctor) · ★2)
- **minni-engine**: Development reference for the Minni engine (v3.1/v3.2). Use when modifying the memory system's extraction, reranking, consolidation, or retrieval pipelines. ([infektyd/minni](https://github.com/infektyd/minni/tree/HEAD/plugins/minni/skills/minni-engine) · ★2)
- **minni-ingestion**: Ingest LLM Wiki pages into Minni with agent-optimized enrichment (frontmatter metadata, wikilink graph extraction, source provenance tagging). Use when setting up, maintaining, or running wiki-aware Minni ingestion. ([infektyd/minni](https://github.com/infektyd/minni/tree/HEAD/plugins/minni/skills/minni-ingestion) · ★2)
- **minni-install**: Use when installing, setting up, repairing, or verifying Minni on the platform you are running in (Claude Code, Codex, Gemini, Grok, KiloCode, or a generic MCP host) — plugin install, per-agent vaults, Layer 1 identity/envelope delivery, MCP wiring, socket/daemon hydration, and drift repair. Formerly named minni-propagation. ([infektyd/minni](https://github.com/infektyd/minni/tree/HEAD/plugins/minni/skills/minni-install) · ★2)

- **mirna-annotation-against-mirbase**: Use when small RNA-seq reads (e.g., 50 nt serum FASTQ with 3' adapter TGGAATTCTCGGGTGCCAAGG) have been STAR-aligned to hg19/hg38 and must be quantified against miRBase miRNAs to build a miRNA-by-sample count matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-annotation-against-mirbase))
- **mirna-database-aggregation**: Use when annotating miRNAs in a plant species with a reference genome and you need a consolidated candidate precursor list combining public database annotations with novel sRNA-Seq-based predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-database-aggregation))
- **mirna-detection-comparison-across-pipelines**: Use when two small RNA-seq miRNA identification pipelines (e.g. COMPSRA and exceRpt) have been run on identical FASTQ inputs and you need to quantify detection overlap and independently validate tool-unique miRNA calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-detection-comparison-across-pipelines))
- **mirna-expression-arm-selection-boxplotting**: Use when manually curating candidate plant miRNAs and needing to inspect arm-specific expression and 5p/3p arm-switching events of a selected precursor across available sRNA-Seq samples, as part of the manualCuration HTML report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-expression-arm-selection-boxplotting))
- **mirna-expression-prediction-from-bulk-mrna**: Use when you have paired bulk mRNA and miRNA expression matrices (genes as rows, samples as columns) and want to predict miRNA expression in samples or pooled single-cell clusters lacking direct miRNA measurement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-expression-prediction-from-bulk-mrna))
- **mirna-hybrid-target-summary-visualization**: Use when you have multiple hyb (and associated fold/vienna) files from replicate miRNA/target ligation experiments (e.g. qCLASH) and need to analyze and plot targets of a defined set of miRNAs across pooled replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-hybrid-target-summary-visualization))
- **mirna-isomir-hierarchical-read-counting**: Use when you have demultiplexed small RNA-seq FASTQ reads plus miRNA reference annotations (GFF3/GTF) and need to distinguish miRNAs from isomiRs at single-nucleotide resolution and test their abundance between conditions (e.g. control vs knockout). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-isomir-hierarchical-read-counting))
- **mirna-marker-ranking-across-normalizations**: Use when scRNAseq clusters must be functionally annotated with miRNA markers via a miRNA-target autoencoder latent space, and you need to know whether normalization of the input counts table changes which markers/scores emerge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-marker-ranking-across-normalizations))
- **mirna-precursor-manual-curation**: Use when annotating miRNAs in a plant species WITH a reference genome, by combining already annotated precursors from miRBase/PmiREN/sRNAanno/PsRNA with new predictions from public sRNA-Seq datasets (SRA accessions or FASTQ), and interactively selecting promising candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-precursor-manual-curation))
- **mirna-read-alignment-with-star**: Use when quality-controlled small RNA-seq reads (e.g., serum FASTQ processed with adapter trimming) must be mapped to hg19/hg38 before annotating mapped reads to miRBase miRNAs and building per-sample miRNA count matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-read-alignment-with-star))
- **mirna-secondary-structure-visualization**: Use when reconstructing or inspecting the manualCuration report generator of iwa-miRNA, or when visualizing a selected plant miRNA precursor's secondary structure, read support, arm expression, and targets in an HTML page. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-secondary-structure-visualization))
- **mirna-segment-identification**: Use when working with hyb-format chimeric RNA records from CLASH/qCLASH (or similar miRNA/target ligation) experiments whose seg1_type, seg2_type, and miRNA_seg annotations are missing, and downstream steps (target analysis, fold analysis, plotting) require knowing which segment is a miRNA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-segment-identification))
- **mirna-selection-rule-encoding**: Use when quantifying miRNAs and isomiRs from small RNA-seq reads with tiny-count and you must decide rule hierarchy, 5' offset, overlap range, and Partial flags for the selection-rules CSV. - **mirna-target-binding-energy-analysis**: Use when a qCLASH/CLASH hybrid-sequence dataset has paired hyb and Vienna (or CT) fold files and the goal is to quantify predicted miRNA-target binding energy and per-nucleotide intermolecular pairing patterns in miRNA-containing hybrids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-target-binding-energy-analysis))
- **mirna-target-bipartite-network-construction**: Use when curating a selected plant miRNA candidate (e.g., ath-MIR156b) in iwa-miRNA and you need to visualize its predicted target interactions as a bipartite network on the manualCuration HTML report page. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mirna-target-bipartite-network-construction))
- **mirna-target-correlation-activity-scoring**: Use when inferring miRNA activity from paired or single-sample mRNA profiles with correlation-to-putative-target scoring (miReact), or when benchmarking such target-only methods against a trained predictor like miRSCAPE. - **mirna-target-count-tabulation**: Use when hyb-format hybrid (chimeric miRNA/target ligation) records must be annotated by segment type and miRNA, then filtered to one miRNA of interest and its targets counted and tabulated per replicate. - **mirna-target-grouped-analysis**: Use when you have miRNA/target hybrid data in hyb format (optionally with fold/vienna files) from multiple experimental replicates and need to quantify targets of a defined miRNA set across pooled replicates with tabular and plotted summaries.
